# Coverage audit — old frontends vs feature inventories

Generated 2026-10-01 by `apps/web_new/e2e/parity/tools/coverage-audit.mjs` (rerunnable; report only).

Sources enumerated: `apps/web/app`, `apps/admin/app`, `apps/space/app`,
`desktop-overlay/apps/web/app`, component trees under `core/components` /
`ce/components` (+ admin/space/desktop equivalents), service classes under
`apps/web/core/services`, `packages/services/src` (+ admin/space/cloud services),
palette/shortcut registrations, redirects and error routes. Cloud overlay:
`/tmp/audit-sync/cloud` (present).

### Totals

| Category | Items | Covered | Explained | Unclaimed |
|----------|-------|---------|-----------|-----------|
| routes | 176 | 175 | 1 | 0 |
| components | 300 | 295 | 5 | 0 |
| service-methods | 608 | 597 | 11 | 0 |
| shortcuts | 47 | 47 | 0 | 0 |
| redirects-errors | 25 | 25 | 0 | 0 |
| **All** | **1156** | **1139** | **17** | **0** |

Matching is layered per item: coverage-checklist path mention, then any
row/checklist text mention (with the matched string quoted as evidence), then
explained/no-row section mention. Anything left is **unclaimed** and must gain
rows (or an explained entry) in its owning area file before sign-off.
